				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {

					}
					SOV = {

				}
			}

				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_mbt days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_mbt days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}

						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}

								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_mbt = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_mbt_serial }

	COLDWAR_ORD_approve_light_fighter = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_proposal_ready
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = SOV
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_fighter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction = 0 }
					}
					SOV = {
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_light_fighter days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_light_fighter days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_light_fighter = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_serial }
							}
						}
						OR = {
							FROM = {
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_fraction = 0 }
							}
							SOV = {
								check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_light_fighter_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
					}

	COLDWAR_ORD_approve_cas = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_proposal_ready
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = SOV
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction = 0 }
					}
					SOV = {
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_cas days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_cas days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_cas = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_serial }
							}
						}
						OR = {
							FROM = {
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_cas_fraction = 0 }
							}
							SOV = {
								check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_cas_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
					}
					FROM = { COLDWAR_ORD_approve_cas = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}

	COLDWAR_ORD_approve_carrier = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_proposal_ready
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = SOV
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_carrier = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction = 0 }
					}
					SOV = {
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_carrier days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_carrier days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_carrier = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_serial }
							}
						}
						OR = {
							FROM = {
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_fraction = 0 }
							}
							SOV = {
								check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_carrier_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
					}
					FROM = { COLDWAR_ORD_approve_carrier = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }

	COLDWAR_ORD_approve_attack_submarine = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_proposal_ready
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = SOV
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_fraction = 0 }
					}
					SOV = {
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_attack_submarine days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_attack_submarine days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {

								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_attack_submarine = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_attack_submarine_serial }
							}
						}

							FROM = {

							}

			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_infrastructure = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_infrastructure_serial }
							}
						}
					}
					FROM = { COLDWAR_ORD_reject_infrastructure = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}

	COLDWAR_ORD_approve_dockyard = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_factory
		allowed = { COLDWAR_ORD_tag = yes }
		fire_only_once = no
		target_root_trigger = { always = yes }
		targets = { UKR BLR EST LAT LIT ARM AZE GEO KAZ KYR TAJ TRK UZB MLV }
		target_non_existing = no
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes COLDWAR_ORD_republic = yes }
		}
		visible = {
			COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
			tag = SOV
			FROM = {
				check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
				check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
			}
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_require_proposal_ready
				FROM = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
					check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
				}
				FROM = {
					ROOT = {
						tag = SOV
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_dockyard = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_serial }
					}
				}
				OR = {
					FROM = {
						check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction = 0 }
					}
					SOV = {
						check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_assistance_heading
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_debit_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_preview = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_support_credit_tt }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_success_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_successful_dockyard days = 180 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_ORD_failure_heading
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_unfulfilled_dockyard days = 180 }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_ORD_shortfall days = 180 }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
						tag = SOV
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_state = 1 }
							check_variable = { ROOT.COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = THIS.id }
						}
						FROM = {
							ROOT = {
								tag = SOV
								COLDWAR_ORD_actor = yes
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_target = PREV.id }
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_review_serial_dockyard = PREV.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_serial }
							}
						}
						OR = {
							FROM = {
								check_variable = { COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_fraction = 0 }
							}
							SOV = {
								check_variable = { var = treasury value = FROM.COLDWAR_ORD_dockyard_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							}
						}
					}
					FROM = { COLDWAR_ORD_approve_dockyard = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { base = 0 }
	}
